# AST Function Index

Generated with GCC 15 frontend AST dumps (`-fdump-lang-raw`) from real CMake compile databases for `build/audit-1d` and `build/audit-3d`, plus focused AST probes for files not reached by those target compile databases. Vendored and submodule paths are excluded; only `src/` source locations are retained. Constructors/destructors may appear with GCC internal names such as `__ct` and `__dt`.

- Translation-unit commands processed: 165
- Focused AST probes: Interpolate2D.hpp, CheckNaN.hpp, ArrayView_2d.hpp, and OpenPMD wrapper succeeded
- Unique AST function source locations: 41500
